### GCC culture & occasions

- **Henna: chemistry and timeline (affects both rendering and skin measurement)** — Natural henna (Lawsonia inermis) stains with lawsone (2-hydroxy-1,4-naphthoquinone), which binds to keratin. The stain starts orange and oxidizes to reddish-brown or maroon over about 24-72 h. On skin it fades over about 1-3 weeks as the outer skin layer sheds; palms and fingertips stain darkest and the back of the hand lighter. On the nail plate the stain lasts until the nail grows out. Fingernails grow about 3.5 mm a month (Yaemsiri et al. 2010, JEADV [verify]), so a stained band moves outward over about 4-6 months. Black 'henna' contains PPD, an allergy risk; it is not natural henna, and the app could carry a safety tip. Approximate stain prototypes (my estimates): fresh #d9772f, 1-2 days #b5532a, mature #8b3a1e, dark #6e2a17, fading on light skin #c99a6b.
- **Henna × polish color interaction** — Under sheer, jelly, milky or 'soap' polishes the orange-brown nail stain shows through and pulls the color warmer and darker: a sheer pink turns peach or coral, a milky white turns dirty beige, and pale lavender turns greyish-brown. Over henna-stained nails, recommend opaque cremes (2-3 coats) or an opaque white or nude base before light shades. Colors that harmonize with fresh or mature henna: reds (#B5121B), burgundy or wine (#7B1E2C), terracotta (#C4623E), copper (#B06A3B), gold (#C9A54C), deep browns (#4B2E24), warm nudes. Colors that tend to clash with fresh orange henna: cool pastels, blue-pinks, icy lilac and mint [opinion]. With fading yellow-orange henna, sheers look patchy, so use an opaque base. Renderer requirement: detect and keep the natural nail's color, including henna stain, and composite translucent polishes over it according to their opacity; never paint as if over a perfect white nail.
- **Henna × skin-tone measurement** — Mask henna pixels before sampling skin (they have a* far above the skin median and C* about 1.5-2.5× skin; see the henna mask method). If more than about 40% of the back of the hand is covered, as with Khaleeji-style designs on the back of the hand and fingers, sample henna-free areas (sides of the fingers, forearm) or ask the user to pick an MST orb manually. Record 'henna present' and its age (fresh, mature, fading) so the recommender favors henna-harmonious shades and opaque formulas.
- **Weddings: milka/malka (marriage contract), henna night, zaffa and reception** — Bridal looks: classic or micro-French, milky white, pink nude, champagne (#E3CBA3), gold chrome or foil to match the heavy gold jewelry traditional in Omani and Gulf bridal sets, red or burgundy for the henna night, and white for a Western-style reception. Brides often have henna 1-3 days before; schedule polish after the stain matures, and suggest opaque formulas over stained nails. Guests: jewel tones (emerald #046A4A, ruby #9B111E, sapphire #0F52BA), gold glitter, rose gold, nudes. Product features: a 'Bridal timeline' planner (henna, then nails, then event) and a bride-plus-bridesmaids group palette. Regional and family customs vary; avoid claiming any one 'traditional' bridal color [opinion].
- **Eid al-Fitr and Eid al-Adha** — These are the peak salon days, especially the last nights of Ramadan and the day before Eid al-Adha. Popular looks [industry or opinion]: gold or rose-gold chrome, glitter, jewel tones, festive reds, and pastels matching new Eid outfits. Children: henna more than polish; if polish, use water-based or peel-off. Approximate dates, subject to Oman's official crescent-sighting announcements: Ramadan about 8 Feb-9 Mar 2027, Eid al-Fitr about 9-10 Mar 2027, Eid al-Adha about 16-17 May 2027 (2026 dates have passed: Eid al-Fitr about 20 Mar 2026 and Eid al-Adha about 27 May 2026) [astronomical estimates; verify]. Use the dates for campaign scheduling and booking-capacity warnings.
- **Ramadan** — Salon hours shift to after iftar, roughly 8 pm to 1 am. Nail polish is not generally regarded as breaking the fast, but its effect on wudu for the five prayers and Taraweeh leads many women to choose henna, removable or peel-off polish, or 'breathable' formulas during Ramadan. Soft, elegant colors for Ramadan gatherings (ghabga) and suhoor events; gold accents. Never infer or ask about menstruation (some women wear regular polish when not praying): offer 'easy-removal' or 'temporary' filters without explaining why.
- **Wudu and 'breathable' or 'halal' polish (handle with great care)** — The common juristic view is that water must reach the nail for wudu (based on Qur'an 5:6), so regular polish, gel, acrylic and press-ons must be removed first. Oman is majority Ibadi Muslim, alongside Sunni and Shia communities, so the app must not issue rulings; point users to their own scholars or official fatwa bodies (for example the Office of the Grand Mufti of Oman). 'Breathable' polishes (Inglot O2M, around 2012-13, was the first widely publicized one; others include Tuesday in Love, 786 Cosmetics, Orly Breathable and Nailberry L'Oxygéné) claim permeability to oxygen or water vapour. Scholars disagree about whether that means water reaches the nail during wudu [verify product names and claims]. 'Halal-certified' usually refers to ingredients (no animal or alcohol derivatives) and is NOT a wudu ruling. UI rule: two separate badges, 'Breathable / water-permeable (manufacturer claim)' and 'Halal-certified ingredients (certifier: X)', plus a 'Wudu-friendly options' filter listing henna, removable or peel-off, and breathable-claim products, with a disclaimer to consult a scholar.
- **Workplace modesty and professional norms** — Ministries, schools, banks and many offices in Oman tend to be conservative. Safe picks: nude and rosy-beige (computed nude ladder), sheer milky pink, mauve (#A87C9F), soft burgundy (#7B1E2C), micro-French, in short-to-medium round, oval or squoval shapes. Avoid neon, long stiletto or coffin shapes and 3D charms for these settings [opinion]. Healthcare and food handling: WHO hand-hygiene guidance (2009) says artificial nails or extenders should not be worn by staff with direct patient contact and nails should be kept short [verify]; many hospitals also ban polish in clinical areas. Offer a 'Clinical / hygiene-safe' mode (natural short nails, care-only services). Many school uniform rules ban polish, which matters for teen customers.
- **Abaya, gold jewelry and outfit coordination** — A black abaya is a neutral backdrop that lets almost any shade stand out. Colored, embroidered and pastel abayas (beige, navy, olive, dusty pink) are popular, so offer 'match to outfit': the user taps a color in a photo of the outfit and gets complementary or tonal polish (same hue with a different lightness, or a complement). Gold-heavy jewelry suits gold or warm metallics and warm reds; silver or diamond sets suit chrome, cool reds and berries.
- **Summer heat, sun and UV (Muscat about May-September)** — Daytime highs often reach 40-45 °C or more, coastal humidity is high and the UV index is extreme (11+) [verify exact climate figures]. Practical effects: regular polish chips faster with heat, sweat, sunscreen, sanitizer and swimming; gel or BIAB lasts longer. Neon or fluorescent pigments and some pinks fade under UV, and non-UV-stabilized white or clear gel top coats can yellow. Summer palette [industry or opinion]: corals, turquoise (#1FA9A0), white or milky, neon (on tan and deep skin), pastels, mint. Rendering caveat: real fluorescent neons glow and lie outside the sRGB gamut, so the screen cannot show them exactly; label such previews 'brighter in person'. Safety: UV/LED nail-lamp exposure caused DNA damage and mutations in cultured cells (Zhivagui et al. 2023, Nature Communications 14:276 [verify]); human risk is unquantified, but suggest hand SPF or fingerless UV gloves. The EU banned TPO photoinitiator in cosmetics, including gel polish, from 1 Sep 2025 (Regulation (EU) 2025/877, 'Omnibus VII') [verify]; GSO/GCC status unknown, so prefer TPO-free gels and label them.
- **National days and seasonal campaigns** — Oman National Day: 20 November (moved from 18 November by royal decree under Sultan Haitham; observed from 2022 or 2023 [verify]). Omani flag colors are red, white and green, so offer flag nail art kits. Neighboring markets: UAE 2 Dec (red, green, white, black), Qatar 18 Dec (maroon and white), Bahrain 16 Dec (red and white), Saudi 23 Sep (green), Kuwait 25-26 Feb (green, white, red, black) [verify]. The large expat communities also celebrate Diwali, Onam, Christmas and Chinese New Year, so seasonal palettes for those groups help.
- **Trends 2025-2026 (unverified this session; refresh with live search)** — Pantone Color of the Year 2025 'Mocha Mousse' 17-1230 (approx #A47864) and 2026 'Cloud Dancer' 11-4201, a soft white (approx #F0EEE9) [from knowledge; verify both, and Pantone's hex values]. Nail trends repeatedly reported in 2025-26 editorials [opinion; verify]: mocha, chocolate and espresso browns; cherry-mocha and burgundy; butter yellow; milky or 'soap' nails; chrome and 'glazed' finishes; cat-eye magnetic; aura or airbrush; jelly; micro-French; pistachio and olive greens; 'blueberry milk' blue. Use them as a 'Trending' tag that a salon admin can update from Firestore, not as fixed code.
- **Photo privacy and modesty** — Many GCC women do not want images of themselves shared. Capture hands only, never faces; process on-device; ask explicit consent before saving or sharing; never post customer hands on salon social media without written consent. Oman's Cyber Crime Law (Royal Decree 12/2011) and PDPL penalize privacy violations through images [verify]. Female-only salon norms: staff devices should not keep customer photos; auto-delete after the session.
- **Language and color naming (Arabic RTL)** — Localize color names with Gulf usage, not just literal translation. Examples [native-speaker review needed]: nude نود / بيج, red أحمر, burgundy عنابي, wine خمري, pink وردي, fuchsia فوشي, mauve موف, lilac ليلكي, navy كحلي, turquoise فيروزي, olive زيتي, gold ذهبي, silver فضي, rose gold روز جولد, off-white سكري, coral مرجاني, brown بني, mocha موكا. Show undertone in friendly terms (warm دافئ, cool بارد, neutral محايد, olive زيتوني) and depth by MST number, avoiding 'fair is better' wording.
- **Religious and mourning periods** — During Muharram and Ashura, some Shia communities in Oman (for example in Muscat) avoid adornment, and families observing mourning ('idda') after a death may avoid cosmetics. Do not push festive campaigns at these times [opinion; consult local staff]. Pilgrims preparing for Umrah or Hajj may book removal services, so offer 'polish removal / natural care' bookings.
- **Children and teens** — Eid and wedding demand includes girls. Provide a kids' mode: water-based, peel-off, non-toxic shades (pastels, glitter); no acetone; henna-safety notice warning against black henna with PPD; no photo storage; guardian present.
- **Skin-tone sensitivity in the GCC** — Colorism and skin-lightening pressure exist in the region. Never present lighter results as 'better'. Use MST numbers, celebrate every depth in marketing imagery (show hands from MST 3-10), and never suggest 'brightening' the customer's skin through the try-on. The renderer must not alter the skin color, only the nails.

### Age

- Do not estimate age from hand photos. Research on hand-based age estimation is thin. 11K Hands (Afifi 2019, Multimedia Tools & Applications 78:20835) includes age metadata (about 190 subjects aged 18-75 per the paper abstract [verify]) but was built for gender and identity work. No validated consumer-grade hand-only age estimator is known. Dorsal-hand ageing signs (wrinkles, lentigines, vein prominence, thinning skin) depend heavily on sun exposure, manual work, genetics, hydration and skin tone: photoageing usually shows later on deeper skin. Error at the individual level would be large. _(ethics: An 'age' label on a beauty service is often hurtful (being told you look older) and nudges people toward ageist 'age-appropriate' colors.)_
- Even face-based age estimation has multi-year errors with demographic gaps. NIST's FATE Age Estimation & Verification evaluations (NIST IR 8525, 2024) reported mean absolute errors of several years, varying by sex, region of birth and image quality [verify]. Hands carry less age information than faces, so expect worse. _(ethics: Errors would not be random: they would cluster by skin tone and ethnicity, which is a fairness risk in a multi-ethnic market (Omani, South Asian, Filipino, Arab expat, Western, East African heritage).)_
- Legal risk in Oman and the GCC. Oman's Personal Data Protection Law (Royal Decree 6/2022, in force February 2023; Executive Regulations by Ministerial Decision 34/2024) treats biometric, genetic and health data, and data showing ethnic origin, as sensitive. Processing them needs a Ministry permit, children's data needs guardian consent, and cross-border transfer is restricted [verify the exact articles]. A hand image of an identifiable person is personal data. Inferred age, or skin-tone data stored with identity, raises the risk. Firebase servers are outside Oman, so uploading images means cross-border transfer. Related laws: UAE PDPL (Federal Decree-Law 45/2021), KSA PDPL (in force 2023, enforced September 2024), Qatar Law 13/2016, Bahrain Law 30/2018 [verify]. If you later expand to the EU, the EU AI Act Art. 5(1)(g) bans biometric categorization that infers race, among other things. This is a legal overview, not legal advice; get local counsel. _(ethics: Privacy by design: run all analysis on-device, never upload hand images by default, store only the confirmed MST level, undertone and preferences with explicit opt-in, and make deletion easy.)_
- Minors: girls commonly get henna or polish for Eid and weddings. Age estimation must never be used to gate or profile children. _(ethics: Ask whether a guardian is present for under-18s through a simple self-declared toggle, recommend kid-safe water-based or peel-off polish, and keep no child images.)_
- SAFER ALTERNATIVE 1, style persona: a 3-question quiz (Classic/Minimal, Soft Glam, Trendy, Bold/Artistic, Bridal/Occasion) plus maintenance level and preferred length. _(ethics: The customer chooses how to be described; there are no inferred demographics.)_
- SAFER ALTERNATIVE 2, occasion and context: work (conservative, clinical), daily, Eid, wedding guest, bride, henna night, vacation or summer, National Day. _(ethics: Occasion predicts the right color better than age does, and it is not sensitive.)_
- SAFER ALTERNATIVE 3, opt-in hand-concern toggles instead of age: 'soften the look of visible veins', 'downplay sun spots or uneven tone', 'nail ridges', 'short or brittle nails', 'make fingers look longer'. _(ethics: These address the same needs people have as they age without labelling anyone, and the customer turns them on herself.)_
- Visible veins (industry advice; opinion): rosy-beige or pink-leaning nudes (#E3A5A0, #D99A94) and warm-neutral reds pull attention away from blue-green veins. Avoid blue, green and violet polishes that repeat the vein color if the customer is self-conscious. Very high-contrast dark shades on fair skin can make veins stand out more by comparison. _(ethics: Present as an optional tip, never a rule.)_
- Sun spots or lentigines (industry advice; opinion): choose shades that do not repeat the color of the spots. Avoid tan, caramel and yellow-beige nudes close to the spot color (#C99A6B-like) and prefer rosy nudes, blue-based reds (#A6192E), berries (#8C1C3A) and soft mauves (#A87C9F), which make skin look brighter by contrast. Blue-based reds counteract yellowness. _(ethics: Advise hand sunscreen as a care tip in Oman's extreme UV, framed as skincare, not anti-ageing pressure.)_
- Finish and texture for mature or ridged nails (industry advice; opinion): cream, gel-shine or jelly finishes over a ridge-filling base. Frost and pearl finishes are often said to highlight ridges because uneven reflection shows texture; fine glitter can hide them (opinions differ). Shorter round, oval or soft-squoval shapes look tidy on thinner skin. Sheer milky pinks (#F3D6D6) blur discoloration. _(ethics: These are aesthetic opinions; shape them by the customer's taste.)_
- Yellowed or stained natural nails (from ageing, smoking, dark-polish staining, or henna): recommend a base coat and opaque cremes over sheers. In the renderer, model the nail plate's measured color under translucent formulas. _(ethics: Describe it neutrally ('to even out the nail color'), with no judgment.)_
- Do not hard-code 'age-appropriate' palettes (for example 'no neon after 40'). Industry advice increasingly calls that outdated. _(ethics: Anti-ageism: every customer gets the full palette; the toggles only re-rank it.)_

## Finishes & product types

This covers nail finishes, materials and product types for a browser WebGL nail try-on aimed at Muscat / GCC. It has 45 finishes, 16 product types and 38 nail-art techniques. Every finish has render parameters on 0..1 scales that map onto the layered physically based rendering (PBR) model used by three.js and glTF. That model has clearcoat, iridescence and sheen layers (Khronos KHR_materials_*), a flake layer (Dassault Enterprise PBR) and the clear-coat attenuation from Google Filament. Glints use real-time glint BRDFs (Chermain 2020; Deliot & Belcour 2023).

KEY FINDINGS
(1) Breathable / 'halal' / wudu-friendly polish matters a lot in Oman, and the evidence is weak.
- Brands make claims and several certifiers are named, but their scope differs: ISNA Canada and HCSC certify Tuesday in Love, according to the brand. The ISWA certificate for Orly Breathable reportedly covers ingredients only, not suitability for ablution. Inglot O2M says it holds a halal certificate and is sold in Muscat malls. Other certifiers named are IFANCA (Maya, reportedly), ISA (Mersi), and GIC or Halal Transactions of Omaha for 786 Cosmetics, where the sources conflict.
- The only independent peer-reviewed study found found no strong evidence that water passes through any of the five polishes tested (Barker et al., J. Cosmet. Sci. 74(6):360–371, 2023, University of Toledo). It also says filter-paper and oxygen-transmission tests do not prove that liquid water reaches the nail.
- A post attributed to Oman's Grand Mufti, Sheikh Ahmed al-Khalili, treats polish as a barrier for any wudu made after applying it.
- Oman's Ministry of Commerce standards store lists GSO 2055-4:2021 (halal cosmetics). That standard does not certify permeability, and no Omani rule was found that makes halal certification of cosmetics mandatory.
- Recommendation: show 'brand claim + certifier + scope + date checked', never say 'wudu-valid', and add a neutral note to consult a trusted scholar. Gel, BIAB, acrylic, dip, polygel and press-ons are not permeable.

(2) Lacquer vs gel look.
- Glossmeter readings put both in the high-gloss range: a coloured nitrocellulose lacquer measured about 88 GU at 60°, and LED gel resins in a patent target at least 80 GU at 60°.
- The deeper look of gel comes from self-levelling (no orange-peel texture) and greater thickness. The simulation should therefore change the coat normal's smoothness, the refraction parallax and the apex shape, not only roughness.
- Measured lacquer film thickness (HD-OCT): 1 coat ≈ 21 µm, 2 coats ≈ 52 µm, 3 coats ≈ 73 µm.
- Acrylic free edge is about one credit card thick (≈0.76 mm) and the apex 2–3 cards, according to practitioners.

(3) Effect-finish physics, mapped to shader terms.
- Pearl: thin film on mica. TiO2 layer thickness sets the colour: silver 40–60 nm → gold/yellow 60–80 → red 80–100 → blue 100–140 → green 120–160 nm (patent data).
- Holo: diffraction-grating flakes. Fine, dense flakes give a linear rainbow; coarser ones give scattered sparkles. It needs a compact light source and looks silver in diffuse light.
- Chrome powder: flat platelets burnished onto a no-wipe gel top coat, giving a true mirror that needs an environment map.
- Cat-eye: magnetic particles aligned into a band. Magnet distance sets band sharpness (about 2–3 mm gives a sharp line, about 5 mm a soft one). Velvet spreads the particles across the whole nail; 9D and crystal are marketing labels.
- Reflective glitter: retroreflection, bright only when the light is near the camera (flash).
- Thermal: leuco-dye microcapsules.
- Crackle: an over-pigmented film (PVC about 30% in one patent) that cracks in 5–10 minutes.
- Matte: silica matting agents roughen the surface.

(4) Trends for 2025–2026.
- Velvet / magnetic cat-eye, especially jewel and deep tones.
- Chrome moving from mirror silver to pearl, glazed and 'invisible' chrome, and to brown / bronze / copper / pewter.
- Jelly and glazed-jelly finishes.
- Milky whites: Pantone 2026 is Cloud Dancer 11-4201; Pantone 2025 was Mocha Mousse 17-1230.
- Aura and sunset ombré, micro-French in espresso, burgundy and copper, and short square shapes.
- In the GCC: gold or chrome for Ramadan and Eid, henna-inspired line art, Islamic geometric patterns, and demand for breathable polish (salon blogs, anecdotal).

LIMITS
- The web-search budget ran out during this work. Some figures are estimates, flagged 'estimate/unverified' in place: gel and BIAB thicknesses, flake sizes for shards and grains, thermal transition temperatures for nail products, the cosmetic regulatory status of glow pigments, the TiO2 refractive index, and the assumed nail width for mm-to-pixel conversion.
- No product swatch hex codes are given. The few colours mentioned are illustrative, not matches to real products.
- Several primary brand sites (Maya, Tuesday in Love, IslamQA, Lab Muffin) were unreachable from this environment, so their claims come from search snippets and should be re-checked before the app shows them.

|---|---|---|---|
| Crème / Cream | كريمي | Opaque, pigment-saturated solid colour with no visible particles. The only highlight is the white reflection from the glossy top coat. Two coats fully hide the nail bed, lunula and free edge. | easy |
| High-gloss (lacquer + glossy top coat) | لامع جداً | Wet, mirror-like coat reflection over any colour. The highlight is crisp (windows and lamps are recognisable) and the Fresnel brightening at the side walls is strong. | easy |
| Gel gloss (cured gel top coat, extra depth) | لمعة الجل (عمق زجاجي) | Thick, self-levelled glassy layer. Reflections are sharp and undistorted (no orange-peel) and there is a slight dome. The colour looks as if it sits under glass, and thin bright rim highlights run along the side walls and free edge. | medium |
| Matte | مطفي (مات) | No mirror highlight, only a broad soft sheen. Colours look slightly lighter and chalkier, and dark shades lose depth. Wear and skin oils polish shiny patches over time. | easy |

| Jelly / sheer | جيلي شفاف | Translucent, saturated, glassy candy colour. The nail bed, lunula and free edge show through. Colour deepens with each coat and where the film is thicker, and the finish is very glossy. Trends: 'glazed jelly' and 'jelly French' (2026). | medium |
| Crelly (cream-jelly) | كريمي جيلي (كريلي) | Semi-opaque 'squishy' colour that mostly covers but keeps soft depth. At one coat the free edge is faintly visible. | easy |
| Milky (milky white / milky pink) | حليبي | Soft, translucent white or off-white veil that blurs the nail bed and smile line. Looks clean, creamy and luminous. Linked to the 2026 Pantone Cloud Dancer white. | easy |
| Sheer French base (sheer pink / nude) | قاعدة فرنسية شفافة | Sheer pink or nude wash that evens out nail-bed redness, softens the white free edge and adds gloss: 'your nails, but better', and the base of a French manicure. | easy |
| Tinted rubber base / cover base | قاعدة مطاطية ملونة | Thick, self-levelling, sheer-to-semi-opaque tint (milky pink, nude, milky white) with a smooth, pronounced apex and gel-level gloss. It camouflages the nail bed. | medium |
| Shimmer | شيمر متلألئ | Colour with fine sub-pixel sparkle that twinkles as the hand moves. The sparkle is tiny points of light, not visible particles. | medium |
| Pearl / pearlescent | لؤلؤي | Soft, satiny lustre: a white-silver or tinted sheen glides across the nail and the colour lightens at the highlight. No distinct sparkles; faint brush strokes possible. | medium |
| Frost | فروست (صقيعي) | Very dense, fine whitish-silver pearl or metallic giving an icy sheen, with visible brush strokes and a streaky highlight along the nail (Y2K / 90s frost). | medium |
| Metallic (gold, silver, rose-gold, bronze, gunmetal) | معدني | Opaque metal look: highlights are tinted by the metal colour, off-specular areas go dark, and fine flake texture shows up close. 2026 shift toward brown, bronze, copper and pewter metallics. | medium |
| Liquid chrome / chrome-effect polish | كروم سائل (طلاء) | Brighter and smoother than metallic but not a true mirror: blurred reflections and slight streaks. | medium |
| Chrome (mirror powder) | كروم مرآة (بودرة) | True mirror that reflects the surroundings (room, lights, fingers) like polished metal, with sharp, continuous reflections. Tints include silver, gold, rose, blue and coloured chrome. | hard |
| Glazed / pearl chrome ('glazed donut', 'invisible chrome') | كروم لؤلؤي (جليزد دونات) | Milky or sheer base under a translucent pearlescent glaze that sweeps a soft white-to-pastel iridescent sheen across the nail. Luminous and wet-looking. 2025–2026 soft and 'invisible' chrome trend. | medium |
| Holographic – linear | هولوغرافي خطي | Under a point light (sun, spotlight, phone torch) a sharp rainbow band radiates from the light's reflection across the nail and moves with tilt. In diffuse light it looks like plain silver. | hard |
| Holographic – scattered | هولوغرافي متناثر | Rainbow sparkles scattered over the whole nail instead of one band. Each particle flashes its own spectral colour, and it is visible in more lighting conditions than linear holo. | hard |
| Aurora / unicorn powder | أورورا / بودرة يونيكورن | Sheer iridescent veil over a base colour. At the highlight it flashes pastel blue, pink, gold or green, and off-angle the base shows through. Chameleon-like over dark bases, pastel over light ones. | medium |
| Opal | أوبال | Milky translucent base with flashes of pink, green, blue or gold from fine iridescent particles suspended at different depths, like opal stone. | hard |
| Glitter – fine | جليتر ناعم | Dense, evenly glittering surface of tiny metallic or holo particles that are barely resolved individually. Strong twinkle; slightly textured unless sealed under a thick coat. | medium |
| Glitter – chunky / mixed | جليتر خشن (قطع كبيرة) | Individually visible pieces of mixed size in a clear or jelly base. Each blinks on its own, pieces overlap, and the surface is bumpy unless encapsulated in gel. | hard |
| Hex glitter (large) | جليتر سداسي | Large hexagonal glitter (holo, iridescent or metallic) with crisp flat facets; each hex flashes as a whole. Reads as party or Eid sparkle. | hard |
| Flakies / shards | رقائق فليكي (شظايا) | Irregular metallic or iridescent shards of varying size floating in a clear or jelly base. Large, angular glints with crinkly edges, like broken glass or foil. | hard |
| Iridescent flakes (mylar / chameleon flakes) | رقائق قزحية (حرباء) | Very thin, translucent, colour-shifting films that flash pink to gold to green as the nail tilts, and turn transparent off-angle so the base shows through. | hard |
| Reflective glitter (flash-reactive) | جليتر عاكس (يلمع مع الفلاش) | In room light it is a fine grey or silver shimmer or plain colour. Under camera flash or a light near the viewer it blazes bright white or rainbow, like road-sign reflectors. | hard |
| Cat-eye / magnetic – single band | عين القطة (مغناطيسي كلاسيك) | Deep base colour with a single sharp, luminous line (vertical or diagonal) that seems to float under the surface and slides across the nail as it tilts, like a cat's pupil or tiger's-eye stone. | medium |
| Velvet cat-eye | عين القطة المخملي | Magnetic particles spread across the whole nail: a soft, hazy, shimmering glow with velvet-fabric depth and a broad light beam or orb instead of a line. Winter 2025–2026 trend in charcoal, midnight blue, plum and burgundy. | medium |
| 9D cat-eye | عين القطة 9D | Multi-dimensional magnetic effect: several layered light bands or a starburst with a 3+ colour multichrome shift and deep holographic-like dimension. | hard |
| Crystal / glass cat-eye | عين القطة الكريستالي/الزجاجي | Magnetic sparkle suspended in a sheer jelly: a glittering band with see-through depth. 'Crystal' leans glittery; 'glass' is jelly-encapsulated. | hard |
| Duochrome | ثنائي اللون (دوكروم) | Two distinct colours depending on viewing angle (for example purple face-on, green toward the edges or when tilted). Strongest over black. | medium |
| Multichrome / chameleon | متعدد الألوان (مالتي كروم/حرباء) | A 3–5+ colour sweep across the nail with tilt (for example teal to blue to purple to red), with intense colour travel over a black base. | hard |
| Neon (fluorescent) | نيون (فسفوري) | Ultra-bright, highly saturated colours (lime, hot pink, orange) that look brighter than normal colours in daylight and glow under UV light. Often dries satin and streaky; best over a white base. | medium |
| Glow-in-the-dark | يتوهج في الظلام | In normal light: pale, translucent whitish, green or blue-tinted, slightly grainy. In darkness: a soft luminous glow that fades over minutes. | medium |

| Crackle / shatter | متشقق (كراكل) | The top layer splits into random islands that expose the base colour underneath (lizard or shatter look). Semi-matte unless top-coated. | medium |

| Regular nail lacquer (air-dry polish) | طلاء أظافر عادي (مناكير) | 3–7 (typical practitioner estimate; depends on prep and top coat) | Thinnest film: HD-OCT measured 1 coat ≈ 21 µm (15–29), 2 coats ≈ 52 µm, 3 coats ≈ 73 µm. It follows nail ridges, which can show at grazing light under cremes. High gloss when fresh (coloured lacquer control about 88 GU at 60° in one patent), but with faint orange-peel and brush texture, a hand-painted cuticle margin (about 0.3–0.5 mm gap; estimate) and no apex. Gloss fades and the tip wears within days. Sim: coat_thickness ≈ 0.05–0.1 mm, clearcoat 0.8–0.95, coat roughness 0.06–0.12, orange-peel normal noise on, no dome, no extension. |
| Breathable / water-permeable ('halal', wudu-friendly) lacquer | طلاء قابل للتنفس/نافذ للماء (حلال) | 3–7 (estimate; brand-dependent) | Same visual class as regular lacquer: a thin film of about 20 µm per coat, usually 2 coats plus the brand's own breathable top coat. Lacquer-level gloss with no gel depth (sim: clearcoat 0.6–0.85, coat roughness 0.08–0.15, orange-peel on). Ranges are mostly creme, sheer, shimmer, pearl and some glitter. Durability may be lower (unverified), so tip-wear simulation is relevant. |
| Water-based / peel-off polish | طلاء مائي قابل للتقشير | 1–3 (estimate) | Softer, slightly thicker-looking film per coat, with lower gloss and less leveling than solvent lacquer (estimate). Edges can lift and show as a thin rim. Sim: gloss 0.7–0.8, clearcoat 0.4, slight edge lift. |
| Gel polish (soak-off soft gel, 'shellac', 'hybrid') | جل بوليش (شيلاك) يُزال بالنقع | 14–21 | Thicker and self-levelled, with no orange-peel. Sharper reflections with visible 'under glass' depth, a slight dome, crisp bright edges, and colour taken close to the cuticle. Hybrid systems are generally thinner and easier to remove than builder systems (dermatology review). Total thickness is about 0.1–0.25 mm (estimate; no measurement found). Gloss holds 2–3 weeks; regrowth gap at the cuticle after about 2 weeks. Sim: clearcoat 1, coat roughness 0.02–0.05, parallax on, dome +10%, cuticle margin about 0.2 mm (estimate). |
| Rubber base gel (clear or tinted 'cover base') | قاعدة مطاطية (رابر بيس) | 14–21 (estimate) | Thicker than gel polish, with a smooth domed apex and a glassy finish. Tints are sheer to semi-opaque and camouflage the nail bed. Thickness about 0.2–0.4 mm (estimate). Sim: clearcoat 1, coat roughness 0.02–0.04, dome +15–20%, parallax on, milky or sheer path. |
| Builder gel / BIAB (Builder In A Bottle) | جل البناء في زجاجة (BIAB) | 21–28 (with infills) | Visible apex and structure: thicker over the stress area, thin at the cuticle and side walls. Medium-thin viscosity, so it self-levels better than jar builders. High-gloss glassy coat; tinted builders give milky or sheer pink natural looks. Thickness about 0.3–0.8 mm at the apex (estimate). Sim: clearcoat 1, dome +25%, apex highlight bending, visible free-edge thickness of about 0.3–0.5 mm (estimate), optional 1–3 mm length extension. |
| Hard gel (file-off builder / sculpting gel) | جل صلب | 14–21 between fills | Very clear, high-gloss, glass-like enhancement that can be sculpted long (almond, coffin, stiletto). Thicker than BIAB, with a defined apex and C-curve, and it can encapsulate glitter or flakes for depth. Thickness about 0.5–1.0 mm (estimate). Sim: clearcoat 1, coat roughness about 0.02, length extension, sculpted shape template, visible free-edge thickness, rim highlight. |
| Acrylic (liquid monomer + polymer powder) | أكريليك | 14–21 between fills | Thickest and most rigid. Practitioner guideline: free edge about one credit card thick (≈0.76 mm, ID-1 card) for salon wear, apex about 2–3 cards, evenly tapered (a study guide cites 1.0–1.5 mm at the free edge). Powders can look slightly less saturated or grainy unless top-coated. Long shapes and a visible thickness at the free edge in side views. Sim: extension length, shape template, edge thickness about 0.8–1.5 mm, strong apex, clearcoat 1 if gel-topped (otherwise buffed gloss about 0.8). |
| Dip powder | بودرة الغمس (ديب) | 14–21 | Built up from several resin-powder layers: thicker than gel polish and generally thinner than acrylic (sources conflict). The cuticle area can look bulkier, and colour can read slightly powdery or less saturated before topping. Glossy hard finish (gloss about 0.9 with top coat). Glitter and holo dips look scattered rather than linear. Sim: coat thickness about 0.2–0.5 mm (estimate), dome +15%, cuticle ledge, slight desaturation. |
| Polygel (acrylic–gel hybrid) | بولي جل | 14–21 between fills | Visually close to hard gel and acrylic: sculpted extensions (often dual forms), defined apex, very smooth after filing, finished with gel colour or top coat. Thickness similar to hard gel (estimate 0.5–1.0 mm). Sim: same as hard gel. |

Longer, crisp free edge and a faint cuticle line. Sim: shape template per finger, uniform thickness about 0.3–0.5 mm (estimate), clearcoat 1, extension length chosen by the user. |
| Press-on nails (pre-made tips) | أظافر لاصقة جاهزة (بريس أون) | 1–3 with tabs; up to ~7–14 with glue (estimate) | Uniform factory shapes. The curvature may not match the natural nail, so side gaps can show and the edge is thicker. Usually longer. Designs are perfectly repeated (factory gloss or matte). Sim: extension template, edge thickness about 0.5–1 mm (estimate), slight misfit gap at the side walls is optional, art printed or encapsulated. |

Lacquer-level gloss, printed or real glitter, possible tiny wrinkles at the side walls and a filed edge. Sim: same as lacquer, with an option for a slight side-wall crease. |

Designs are printed (flat): printed 'chrome' or 'glitter' lacks real 3D sparkle unless the strip contains real glitter. Clean filed free edge. Sim: gel coat, art as a flat albedo or roughness map, sparkle only where real particles exist. |
